61:     public ReplayStats replay(long runId, String symbol, Instant executionStart, Instant executionEnd,
62:                               List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals,
63:                               List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents,
64:                               BooleanSupplier stopRequested) {
65:         List<TradeSignal> timeline = generatedSignals.stream()
66:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
67:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt)
68:                         .thenComparingInt(s -> intervalOrder(s.getInterval())))

72:         TradeSignal latest5m = null;

76:         BigDecimal realized = BigDecimal.ZERO;
77:         int trades = 0, wins = 0, losses = 0;

87:         // transparently retain candle/signal-close fallback and are therefore not claimed
88:         // as tick-exact parity runs.
89:         List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> livePrices = productionPriceEvents == null
90:                 ? List.of()

92:                     .filter(e -> e != null && e.observedAt() != null && e.price() != null)
93:                     .sorted(Comparator.comparing(MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent::observedAt))
94:                     .toList();
95:         boolean exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty();
96:         int livePriceIndex = 0;
97:         // FIX-109: normal Regression/Replay runs are Production-Parity by default.
98:         // Experimental rules require an explicitly opened EXPERIMENTAL scope.
99:         String replayLogicMode = ExecutionReplayScope.ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY.name();
100:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunStart(runId, symbol);
101:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.startRun(runId, symbol);
102: 
103:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, timeline,
104:                 opportunity -> {
105:                     TradeSignal latest = opportunity == null ? null : opportunity.getLatestSignal();

107:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
108:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {

117:         for (TradeSignal signal : timeline) {
118:             // FIX-090: shadow execution is frequently cancellable without affecting Production.
119:             if (stopRequested != null && stopRequested.getAsBoolean()) {
120:                 throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
121:             }
122:             // FIX-052 exact ordering: BinanceKlineService invokes live position protection
123:             // from the 1m price update BEFORE publishing the candle-close analysis event.
124:             // Consume all persisted Production prices up to this signal timestamp before
125:             // making the freshly generated signal visible to position management.
126:             while (livePriceIndex < livePrices.size()
127:                     && (stopRequested == null || !stopRequested.getAsBoolean())
128:                     && !livePrices.get(livePriceIndex).observedAt().isAfter(signal.getGeneratedAt())) {
129:                 MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent live = livePrices.get(livePriceIndex++);
130:                 replayScope.referencePrice(symbol, live.observedAt(), live.price());
131:                 // FIX-11Q: advance only the isolated counterfactual grace position. This never mutates
132:                 // the baseline Shadow position/cash/trades and therefore cannot change Replay parity.
133:                 oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
134:                 if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
135:                     LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
136:                     open = protection.position();
137:                     if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
138:                         cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
139:                         realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
140:                     }
141:                     ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
142:                     if (liveExit.exit()) {
143:                         BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
144:                         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
145:                         BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
146:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
147:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
148:                         cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
149:                         realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
150:                         trades++;
151:                         if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
152:                         // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
153:                         // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
154:                         persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);

156:                         replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());

159:                     }
160:                 }
161:             }

789:     public void markExecuted(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision decision) {
790:         if (signal == null || decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return;
791:         currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
792:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
793:                     boolean stillBuilding = "SCOUT_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
794:                             || "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
795:                             || "CONFIRMATION_ADD".equals(decision.source());
796:                     opportunity.setStatus(stillBuilding ? "BUILDING" : "EXECUTED");
797:                     opportunity.setExecutionSource(decision.source());
798:                     opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(decision.positionPercent());
799:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode(decision.code());
800:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(decision.explanation());
801:                     opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
802:                     opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
803:                     if (!stillBuilding) {
804:                         opportunity.setExecutedAt(Instant.now());
805:                     }
806:                     opportunity.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
807:                     saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
808:                 });
809:     }

812:     /**
813:      * FIX-020 / ENAUSDT 2026-08-20:
814:      * A fully closed position is an immutable evidence boundary. Scout/probe/confirmation
815:      * opportunities intentionally remain BUILDING while the SAME position is open, but once
816:      * that position reaches any terminal exit (TP, SL, SIGNAL_SELL, profit lock, manual close),
817:      * its pre-exit evidence may never finance a brand-new position. Replay calls this exact
818:      * method too, so production and Proven/Regression share the same lifecycle semantics.
819:      */
820:     @Transactional
821:     public void completePositionOpportunity(String symbol, TradeSignal exitSignal, String closeReason) {
822:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) return;
823:         currentOpportunity(symbol, List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
824:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
825:                     opportunity.setStatus("COMPLETED");
826:                     if (exitSignal != null) {
827:                         opportunity.setLatestSignal(exitSignal);
828:                         if (exitSignal.getGeneratedAt() != null) {
829:                             opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(exitSignal.getGeneratedAt());
830:                         }
831:                     }
832:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode("POSITION_CLOSED_EVIDENCE_BOUNDARY");
833:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(
834:                             "Position lifecycle completed"
835:                                     + (closeReason == null || closeReason.isBlank() ? "" : " via " + closeReason)
836:                                     + "; pre-exit opportunity evidence is consumed and cannot justify a new position.");
241:                              + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour
242:                             + ". 1m=" + executionSignal.getDecision()
243:                             + ", confidence=" + executionSignal.getConfidenceScore()
244:                              + ", 5m=NEUTRAL, 1h=" + oneHour
245:                             + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
246:                             + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
247:                             + ", position=25%.");
248:         }
249: 
250:         // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
251:         // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
252:         // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
253:         // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
254:         // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
255:         // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
256:         // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
257:         // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
258:         //
259:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
260:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
261:         // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
262:         // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
263:         boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
264:         if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
265:             boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
266:             boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
267:             // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.
268:             boolean btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
269:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
270:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
271: 
272:             if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {
273:                 log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
274:                                 + "decision={}, confidence={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, btcStatus={}, positionPercent=25",
275:                         executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
276:                         executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),
277:                         fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore, btcStatus);
278:                 return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H",
279:                         "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H: 25% exploratory entry approved for an already-approved "
280:                                 + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "

300:     private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
301: 
302:         if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
303:             return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
304:                     "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
305:         }
306:         if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
307:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
308:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
309:         }
310:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
311:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
312:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
313:         }
314:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
315:             return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
316:                     "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
317:         }
318: 
319:         return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
320:                 "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
321:     }

340:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
341:                     "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "

350:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
351:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
352:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
353:         }
354:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
355:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
356:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
357:         }
358:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
359:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
360:         }
361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
363:         }
364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
365:     }

99:     @Async
100:     public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
101:         // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
102:         // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
103:         if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
104:             log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
105:             return;
106:         }
107:         // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
108:         // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
109:         // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.

import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;

/** FIX-138: reserve live capacity; historical catch-up cannot consume it.
 * No caller-runs fallback: a saturated pool must never execute on a dispatch clock. */
@Configuration
public class SharedAnalysisExecutorConfig {
    // FIX-142: dispatcher and executor receive the same validated historical setting.
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.historical-threads:1}")
    private int historicalThreads=1;
    @Bean("sharedLiveAnalysisExecutor")
    public ThreadPoolTaskExecutor live() { return pool(8, "shared-live-"); }

    @Bean("sharedHistoricalAnalysisExecutor")
    public ThreadPoolTaskExecutor historical() { if(historicalThreads<1||historicalThreads>4)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Historical capacity must be 1..4");return pool(historicalThreads, "shared-history-"); }

    private ThreadPoolTaskExecutor pool(int size, String prefix) {
        var executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
        executor.setCorePoolSize(size);
        executor.setMaxPoolSize(size);
        // At most one admitted task per thread; the small queue only accommodates
        // the interval between a task releasing its lane and returning to the pool.
        executor.setQueueCapacity(size);
        executor.setThreadNamePrefix(prefix);
        executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
        executor.setWaitForTasksToCompleteOnShutdown(true);
        executor.setAwaitTerminationSeconds(30);
        return executor;
    }
}
package com.crypto.config;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor;

import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;

/**
 * FIX-043: dedicated executor for closed-candle analysis.
 *
 * Production evidence on 22 Aug 2026 showed 96.7-100% 1m candle persistence but only
 * 17-20% technical_indicator/trade_signal coverage. The old AFTER_COMMIT listener ran the
 * complete indicator -> signal -> wallet path synchronously on the Binance message thread.
 * When that work took longer than the incoming market-data cadence, the websocket consumer
 * could not keep up and the recovery scheduler became the de-facto analysis clock.
 *
 * CandleAnalysisDispatcher supplies serial bounded admission per symbol+interval while this pool supplies
 * parallelism across independent streams. The pool sizes are configuration-driven because the
 * correct concurrency depends on VPS CPU/DB capacity; changing them must never change trading
 * decisions, only how quickly independent market streams can be analyzed.
 *
 * FIX-153: saturation rejects admission; the dispatcher releases its reservation and the
 * local detector can retry. Analysis must never execute on a Binance ingestion thread.
 *
 * IMPORTANT: this class must never contain BUY/SELL scoring, execution thresholds,
 * SETUP_CONFIRMATION_WAKEUP, ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE or exit logic.
 */
@Configuration
public class CandleAnalysisAsyncConfig {

    @Bean(name = "candleAnalysisExecutor")
    public Executor candleAnalysisExecutor(
            @Value("${trading.analysis-worker-core-size:8}") int coreSize,
            @Value("${trading.analysis-worker-max-size:16}") int maxSize,
            @Value("${trading.analysis-worker-queue-capacity:200}") int queueCapacity
    ) {
        int safeCoreSize = Math.max(1, coreSize);
        int safeMaxSize = Math.max(safeCoreSize, maxSize);
        ThreadPoolTaskExecutor executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
        executor.setCorePoolSize(safeCoreSize);
        executor.setMaxPoolSize(safeMaxSize);
        executor.setQueueCapacity(Math.max(1, queueCapacity));
        executor.setThreadNamePrefix("candle-analysis-");
        executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
        executor.setWaitForTasksToCompleteOnShutdown(true);
        executor.setAwaitTerminationSeconds(30);
        executor.initialize();
        return executor;
    }
}

It must never claim that it reproduces historical wallet state, live scheduling, MySQL locks, Binance availability, or actual fills.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The preparation is not freeze-ready. It explicitly leaves P01–P07 pending, including the exact baseline, evidence E03–E10, canonical specification, approval, and guard proofs. The canonical path is still RPR-0.4, not the proposed RPR-0.9.12. [Preparation gates](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:53) [canonical specification](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:1)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The no-Production-touch rule is not implementable from this preparation as written. R156-05 assigns work to `ExecutionReplayScope`, a shared Spring component used by execution, validation, market-context, confluence, and FIX-154 services; R156-06 ambiguously assigns the shared price-event projection. Neither item has an exact Replay-only replacement/file registry. [R156-05/06](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:72) [shared scope](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:17)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current Replay cannot satisfy O-1 without a decided replacement path. It calls shared `evaluateBuy`, which calls `validateBuy`; that validation loads `wallet_settings`. Replay also uses the shared Binance minimum-order service, which calls the Binance client. [Replay BUY call](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:270) [wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:62) [live minimum-order call](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:29)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** A Replay-local mirror can meet the owner rule, but the preparation must identify every mirrored method and its source-version/fingerprint comparison. “Match recorded allow/reject decisions” detects a fixture mismatch; it does not by itself detect future Production-policy drift.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Analysis parity is under-specified. `analyzeForRegression` remains in shared `AnalysisService` and uses current service wiring for sentiment enablement, fundamentals, ATR, regime state, market context, strategy, BTC context, derivatives, and order-book logic. D53/D54 cover only two inputs; the preparation does not contain a complete input-to-fixture/refusal registry for this graph. [analysis graph](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:227)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Equal-time ordering is currently unreconstructable in Replay: SQL orders price rows by `(observed_at, id)`, but the returned `PriceEvent` discards both `id` and `delivery_status`; Replay then sorts only by time. R156-06 identifies the intended repair, but it must be a new Replay-owned reader/DTO—not an edit to the shared service—under the owner rule. [shared projection](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118) [Replay sort](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:89)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The required precedence oracle is absent from the preparation: T156-02a/b reference “Section 3.3.1,” but that section does not exist in the document. This leaves TP/stop/profit-lock/rebase/near-TP ordering non-testable from the frozen artifact. [dangling references](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:167)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The proposed dedicated Replay executor needs an explicit qualified `@Async` design. The current worker uses unqualified `@Async`, while the application already defines multiple `Executor` beans. [worker](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:99) [existing executors](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/config/SharedAnalysisExecutorConfig.java:14)

- **EXTERNAL RESEARCH:** Spring Boot’s current official task-execution documentation states that custom `Executor` beans affect auto-configured async execution and that `@EnableAsync` resolution requires deliberate configuration when multiple executors exist. Publication date: not stated; page currently identifies Spring Boot 4.1.1. Finding: a new unqualified Replay executor could alter existing async resolution. Limitation: this project declares 4.1.0 and no context test was run. Relevance: R156-02 must require `@Async("replayExecutor")`, non-default candidate behavior, and a Spring-context regression test. [Task Execution and Scheduling](https://docs.spring.io/spring-boot/reference/features/task-execution-and-scheduling.html)

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Claude’s claim that only two mechanisms exist for O-1 is too narrow. Replay-local adapters/mirrors, read-only projections, and unmodified shared pure policies are distinct implementation patterns. The actual blocker is that the preparation does not choose and register one per call path.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Claude’s deployment objection is not yet a FIX-156 design defect. A Replay change would require a later build/deployment to be used, but this task authorizes neither deployment nor Production change. If deployment becomes proposed, it needs a separate release-safety review; it cannot be assumed now.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** R156-13 proposes Replay schema/config in the shared application/datasource. Whether a Flyway migration in that schema violates “no Production touch” is unresolved and must be decided before freeze. [R156-13](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:80)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not accept a parity percentage until the missing precedence oracle, all as-of input provenance, availability time, coverage denominator, exclusions, and sealed acceptance protocol are frozen. Otherwise selective refusals and current-state leakage can inflate apparent parity.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, Production system, runtime service, or private logs were accessed; no tests were executed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The external RPR-0.9.12 source is not present in the workspace, so its complete D01–D29 and S01–S14 rows cannot be independently verified here.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** It is unproven that all Replay-reachable paths avoid persistence of regenerated `TradeSignal` entities or shared-table writes when shared-market mode is enabled.

## FINDINGS FOR JEAN

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not freeze FIX-156 under “Replay-only, zero Production-code touch” yet.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Require an exact file-level registry that marks every changed/new file Replay-owned and rejects edits to shared/Production source, including `ExecutionReplayScope`, `MarketPriceEventService`, `AnalysisService`, and validation/execution services.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Require a chosen per-path mechanism: Replay mirror, unmodified pure policy, or refusal. Add source-version drift detection, exact qualified async execution, and an explicit ruling on shared-schema migration.

VERDICT: REJECTED
